In these organisms, HisG does not function alone but forms a heteromultimer complex with HisZ, a pseudo-enzyme that acts as an allosteric regulatory subunit.<br><br>- HisF is a completely different enzyme (imidazoleglycerol-phosphate dehydratase) and does not associate with HisG in a subunit complex.<br><br>- The HisG-HisZ complex in Bacillus subtilis forms an octameric complex (HisG₈Z₈ or similar stoichiometry), where HisZ provides allosteric regulation through feedback inhibition by histidine.<br><br>Therefore, this HisG protein functions as part of a heteromultimer with HisZ subunits.<br><br>2. answer: B</td>
